Both candidates need the DeepGEMM host library rebuilt, so build one image
with the host series extended by sm90-prefill-stage4.patch
(DG_SM90_PREFILL_STAGE4, default off) and sm90-decode-prepare.patch
(DG_SM90_DECODE_PREPARE, default off); all six host patches apply in order
on DeepGEMM v0.1.5. The runtime overlays add the Decode prepare header
change and the cluster router gate (SGLANG_DECODE_ROUTER=cluster), both
enabled only by the in-job Decode prologue selection.

Next: a one-node kernel-bench run compares the four-stage Prefill
candidate with the control on this image; then one serving run uses the
Decode selection and, if it wins the bench, the four-stage switch.

Merges perf/prefill-megamoe-stage4-20261028 (745d681) and
perf/decode-moe-prologue-20261028 (448cf82) onto main ba93f03; the only
conflicts were the host series and the numeric gate's case lists, where
both additions are kept. Full CPU suite and ruff pass.
